## `AFG-1030-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Sterility Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Fruit Patulin Mycotoxin & Pectin Methylesterase Lab
- **Intro:** Sterility Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Fruit Patulin Mycotoxin & Pectin Methylesterase Lab — Pre-Calibration System Verification: Temperature: 25.0 ± 0.5 °C; Humidity: 45 ± 5 %RH; HPLC pressure: 10.0 ± 0.5 MPa; Baseline drift: < 0.5 % over 10 minutes.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 25.0 ± 0.5 °C; Humidity: 45 ± 5 %RH; HPLC pressure: 10.0 ± 0.5 MPa; Baseline drift: < 0.5 % over 10 minutes.
- **Standards:** `AFS 2015`, `ISO 8128-1:2001`, `ISO 5555:2001`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`
